style low L17
Minor: When caller omits `className`, the rendered className is `"nm-icon-btn "` (with trailing space). Harmless cosmetically, but worth trimming (e.g. `` `nm-icon-btn${className ? ` ${className}` : ""}` `` or use clsx) for tidiness.
Existing Code

Suggested Change
<button type={type} className={["nm-icon-btn", className].filter(Boolean).join(" ")} {...rest}>

other low L461-L476
Icon-only buttons rely solely on `title` for the accessible name. The decorative icon is `aria-hidden`, so the button has no text content. `title` is not consistently read by screen readers—add `aria-label="编辑"` / `aria-label="删除"` to give the button a reliable accessible name (current pattern in the file relies on `title` only, so this is a minor concern but a worthwhile improvement).

src/ui/main.css 13 comments
other medium L220-L223
`.nm-input:focus` removes the default focus outline and only swaps border-color to `var(--brand)`. On the new near-white surface this is a very weak focus cue, especially at the small sizes used in McpPanel (text-xs / font-mono). Add an explicit `:focus-visible` ring (e.g. `box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--brand) 25%, transparent)`) so keyboard users get a visible focus indicator per WCAG 2.4.7.
Existing Code

222    border-color: var(--brand);
223  }
Suggested Change
.nm-input:focus-visible {
    outline: none;
    border-color: var(--brand);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--brand) 25%, transparent);
  }
other medium L208-L210
`.nm-icon-btn` defines `:hover`, `:active`, `:disabled` but no `:focus` / `:focus-visible` state. The companion `src/ui/IconButton.tsx` doesn't add focus styling either — icon buttons are typically used in toolbars/headers where there is no surrounding color to differentiate a focused button. Keyboard tab order through icon buttons will be invisible. Add a `:focus-visible` rule so tab focus is discoverable.

maintainability medium L159-L165
`.nm-inset` now relies entirely on `var(--inset-bg)` (rgba 0.045) plus a 1px border for its "凹下/选中" reading. On the near-white `--surface: #fafbfd` this 4.5% dark overlay is barely perceptible, weakening the selected/pressed affordance that many call sites depend on (view toggles in App.tsx:627-670, the 推理强度/字号 selectors in SettingsPage, KanbanBoard drop zones, etc.). Compared with the old dual inset shadow, this collapses a major semantic distinction. Either bump light-mode `--inset-bg` opacity (~0.08) or pair `.nm-inset` with a stronger border (`var(--edge-strong)`) so the depressed state is unambiguously distinguishable from the resting state.

other medium L35
The new light-theme `--t6: #bcc5d6` against `--bg: #eef1f6` / `--surface: #fafbfd` yields a contrast ratio around 1.3:1 — far below WCAG AA's 4.5:1 for text. The token is named "幽灵提示" but is consumed at real sizes for real helper text (SettingsPage.tsx uses `text-[10px] text-[var(--t6)]` in 8 places; TodoCard uses `text-xs text-[var(--t6)]` for interactive hover links). Either darken this value (e.g. `#8e99b0`) or split into two tokens — one for decoration, one for guaranteed-readable ghost text.

Suggested Change

maintainability low L146-L151
`.nm-card-hover:hover` lost its `translateY(-3px) scale(1.01)` lift — the hover state is now a pure `background-color` + `border-color` swap with no transform or shadow change. The diff's design comment explicitly forbids the lift ("无弹跳"), but for the primary draggable/clickable cards (TodoCard, SortableTaskCard, SortableWorkspaceCard, WorkspacePage) the hover discoverability will feel flat. Consider swapping to `box-shadow: var(--shadow-lg)` on hover so the card visibly raises, or a 1–2px translateY combined with `--shadow-lg`, without crossing into the "弹跳" territory.

style low L238-L250
Sidebar panel background transitions still use 0.28s while the rest of the system adopted the new 150ms-card / 100ms-button rhythm. The 0.28s matches the body's theme-switch transition, so on a manual theme toggle the sidebar visibly lags behind the body fade — visually inconsistent with the documented "150ms / 100ms / 无弹跳" discipline. Unify on `0.15s` for background-color; the body-level rule already handles the theme-switch smoothness at the global level.

maintainability low L132-L135
The new `--r-sm / --r-md / --r-lg` border-radius tokens are documented as "新组件取 var；存量 Tailwind rounded-*/text-* 不动", but the component classes themselves hard-code Tailwind rounded utilities (`@apply rounded-2xl`, `rounded-xl`, `rounded-lg`). If the design system wants to globally retune corner radii, the component classes will still need hand-edits. Either wire them up to the tokens (`@apply rounded-[var(--r-lg)]`) so the system is truly token-driven, or drop the tokens if they're strictly for new code only — currently they sit in a half-applied state.

bug medium L133-L135
`@apply rounded-2xl` hard-codes the corner radius even though the comment in the same file says `--r-lg` should be the new system token. Same for `.nm-inset`/`.nm-outset` (`@apply rounded-xl` ≈ `--r-md` 12px) and `.nm-btn` (`@apply rounded-lg` ≈ `--r-sm` 8px). Future radius retunes will require touching every component class. Replace the literal Tailwind classes with the corresponding CSS var so the design tokens actually take effect.

bug medium L208-L210
Disabled `.nm-icon-btn` and `.nm-input` still respond to `:hover` / `:focus` because the disabled rules only change `opacity`, while `:hover` (and `:focus` for inputs) lives in a separate rule with the same specificity and earlier source order. Result: hovering a disabled icon button still darkens the icon and shows the hover background; focusing a disabled input still swaps the border to `--brand`. Disable interaction visuals explicitly with `:disabled:hover` / `:disabled:focus` resets, plus `cursor: not-allowed`.
Existing Code
208.nm-icon-btn:disabled {
209    opacity: 0.5;
210  }
Suggested Change
.nm-icon-btn:disabled {
    opacity: 0.5;
    cursor: not-allowed;
  }
  .nm-icon-btn:disabled:hover {
    color: var(--t4);
    background-color: transparent;
  }

`.nm-input:disabled` has the same problem as `.nm-icon-btn:disabled`: focus styling still applies (border becomes `--brand`), and there's no `cursor: not-allowed`. Add `:disabled:focus` to reset the border back to `--edge`, and set `cursor: not-allowed`.

maintainability medium L83-L86
Component classes transitioned at 100ms (buttons / inputs / inset / outset / icon-btn) and 150ms (cards) but the body's own theme-switch fade runs at 280ms (`body { transition: background-color 0.28s ease, color 0.28s ease; }`). On a `.dark` toggle the components "settle" before the body bg/color finishes fading — for ~130ms the cards/buttons sit against a still-moving background. Either align the body with the card rhythm (150ms) or extend the component transitions to match (280ms) so the theme switch feels unified.
Existing Code

52|/** 一行一个参数 → string[]（trim、去空行） */
53|function parseArgsLines(text: string): string[] {
54|  return text
55|    .split("\n")
56|    .map((l) => l.trim())
57|    .filter((l) => l.length > 0);
58|}
59|
60|/** KEY=VALUE / Key: Value 行 → Record 通用内核（首个分隔符切；缺分隔符行取整行为 key、空值） */
61|function parseKeyValueLines(text: string, sep: string): Record<string, string> {
62|  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
63|  for (const line of text.split("\n")) {
64|    const t = line.trim();
65|    if (!t) continue;
66|    const i = t.indexOf(sep);
67|    if (i < 0) {
68|      out[t] = "";
69|    } else {
70|      out[t.slice(0, i).trim()] = t.slice(i + 1).trim();
71|    }
72|  }
73|  return out;
74|}
75|
76|/** 一行一个 KEY=VALUE → Record */
77|const parseEnvLines = (text: string) => parseKeyValueLines(text, "=");
78|
79|/** Key: Value 行 → Record（HTTP 头格式） */
80|const parseHeaderLines = (text: string) => parseKeyValueLines(text, ":");
81|
82|/** 命令行展示（B0 评审 HIGH）：含空白的参数加引号，让展示 token 与实际 argv 对齐
83| *（argv 是单 token 的 "hello world" 不能被展示成两个 shell 形态的词） */
84|function displayCommandLine(command: string | null | undefined, args: string[]): string {
85|  return [command ?? "", ...args]
86|    .map((a) => (/\s/.test(a) ? JSON.stringify(a) : a))
87|    .join(" ");
88|}
89|
90|/** 头值打码（B0-4）：确认弹窗只露末 4 位，不展示完整鉴权机密 */
91|function maskHeaderValue(v: string): string {
92|  const t = v.trim();
93|  return t.length <= 8 ? "••••" : `••••••${t.slice(-4)}`;
94|}
95|
96|/** 超时输入解析（B0-4）：空 = 默认；非法/越界报错（后端 u64 反序列化对
97| * 负数/小数会裸抛 serde 错误，前端先钳为 5–600 整数） */
98|function parseTimeoutSecs(raw: string): { value: number | null; error?: string } {
99|  const t = raw.trim();
100|  if (!t) return { value: null };
101|  const n = Number(t);
102|  if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 5 || n > 600) {
103|    return { value: null, error: "超时须为 5–600 的整数秒" };
104|  }
105|  return { value: n };
106|}
107|
108|/** 状态点：connected 绿 / down 红（tooltip 带错误）/ absent 灰 */
109|function StateDot({ status }: { status: McpServerStatus }) {
110|  const map: Record<string, { bg: string; tip: string }> = {
111|    connected: { bg: "bg-emerald-500", tip: "已连接" },
112|    down: { bg: "bg-red-500", tip: `不可用：${status.error ?? "未知错误"}` },
113|    absent: { bg: "bg-neutral-400", tip: status.enabled ? "未连接（启动中或等待）" : "已禁用" },
114|  };
115|  const m = map[status.state] ?? map.absent;
116|  return <span className={`size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full ${m.bg}`} title={m.tip} />;
117|}

119|/** 行内错误摘要：stderr 尾巴可能有多行（评审口径），列表行只显示前 80 字符 */
120|function errorSummary(err: string | null | undefined): string {
121|  const oneLine = (err ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
122|  return oneLine.length > 80 ? `${oneLine.slice(0, 80)}…` : oneLine;
123|}

196|export function McpPanel() {
197|  const [servers, setServers] = useState<McpServerStatus[]>([]);
198|  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState | null>(null); // null = 表单收起

200|  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

204|  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

211|    },
212|    []
213|  );

217|    if (noticeTimer.current) clearTimeout(noticeTimer.current);

219|  }, []);

222|    try {

224|      setServers((await invoke<McpServerStatus[]>("mcp_status")) ?? []);
225|    } catch (e) {
226|      handleCommandError(e, "mcp_status", { silent: true });
227|      setError(formatCommandError(e));

230|
231|  /** 800ms 延迟刷新（重连异步收敛）：单句柄防抖（B0 评审：数组累积无上界），卸载清理 */
232|  const refreshTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
233|  useEffect(
234|    () => () => {
235|      if (refreshTimer.current) clearTimeout(refreshTimer.current);
236|    },
237|    []
238|  );
239|  const scheduleRefresh = useCallback(() => {
240|    if (refreshTimer.current) clearTimeout(refreshTimer.current);
241|    refreshTimer.current = setTimeout(refresh, 800);
242|  }, [refresh]);
243|
244|  useEffect(() => {

248|      refresh();
249|    });
250|    return () => {

252|    };

254|
255|  /** 编辑回填 in-flight 闸（B0 评审：连点两行会双 invoke 竞态 setForm——按钮的
256|   * disabled 要等首个 invoke 落地才生效，ref 闸同步无窗口） */
257|  const editLoadingRef = useRef(false);
258|  const startEdit = async (s: McpServerStatus) => {
259|    if (editLoadingRef.current || form !== null) return;
260|    editLoadingRef.current = true;
261|    setError("");
262|    // 表单回填需要原始配置字段——status 视图没有 args/env，
263|    // 从「编辑即整行覆盖」的角度只需回填用户可感知字段，args/env 留空会丢：
264|    // 因此编辑走 invoke bot_get_config 拿完整 mcpServers。
265|    try {

267|      const full = (cfg.mcpServers ?? []).find((x) => x.id === s.id);
268|      if (!full) {
269|        setError("找不到该服务器的完整配置");
270|        return;
271|      }
272|      setForm({
273|        id: full.id,
274|        name: full.name,
275|        transport: full.transport,
276|        command: full.command ?? "npx",

278|        envText: Object.entries(full.env ?? {})

282|          .map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`)
283|          .join("\n"),
284|        url: full.url ?? "",
285|        timeoutSecs: full.timeoutSecs != null ? String(full.timeoutSecs) : "",
286|        enabled: full.enabled,
287|      });
288|    } catch (e) {
289|      handleCommandError(e, "bot_get_config", { silent: true });
290|      setError(formatCommandError(e));
291|    } finally {
292|      editLoadingRef.current = false;
293|    }
294|  };
295|
296|  /** 表单 → McpServerConfig（前端轻校验；后端权威校验兜底） */
297|  const buildPayload = (): McpServerConfig | null => {
298|    if (!form) return null;
299|    const name = form.name.trim();
300|    if (!name) {
301|      setError("名称不能为空");
302|      return null;
303|    }
304|    // stdio 的 command 来自白名单下拉（MCP_STDIO_LAUNCHERS）恒非空，无需守卫
305|    //（B0 评审：原 !form.command 分支不可达，删除）
306|    if (form.transport !== "stdio" && !form.url.trim()) {
307|      setError("http 服务器必须填写端点 URL");
308|      return null;
309|    }
310|    const timeout = parseTimeoutSecs(form.timeoutSecs);
311|    if (timeout.error) {
312|      setError(timeout.error);
313|      return null;
314|    }
315|    return {
316|      id: form.id,
317|      name,
318|      transport: form.transport,
319|      command: form.transport === "stdio" ? form.command : null,
320|      args: form.transport === "stdio" ? parseArgsLines(form.argsText) : [],
321|      env: form.transport === "stdio" ? parseEnvLines(form.envText) : {},
322|      url: form.transport === "http" ? form.url.trim() : null,
323|      headers: form.transport === "http" ? parseHeaderLines(form.headersText) : {},
324|      timeoutSecs: timeout.value,
325|      enabled: form.enabled,
326|    };
327|  };
328|
329|  const requestSave = () => {
330|    setError("");
331|    const payload = buildPayload();
332|    if (payload) setPending(payload); // 打开确认弹窗（拍板 3A）
333|  };
334|
335|  const confirmSave = async () => {

351|    } finally {
352|      setBusy(false);

354|  };

356|  const remove = async (s: McpServerStatus) => {
357|    if (busy) return;
358|    if (!window.confirm(`删除 MCP 服务器「${s.name}」？其工具将立即从机器人清单消失。`)) return;
359|    setBusy(true);
360|    setError("");
361|    try {

365|    } catch (e) {

367|      setError(formatCommandError(e));
368|    } finally {
369|      setBusy(false);
370|    }
371|  };

376|    setError("");

381|    } catch (e) {

389|  /** 展开/拉取工具：请求序号防串台（B0 评审：连点两台服务器，慢响应会把
390|   * A 的工具渲染进 B 的展开行）；失败在展开行内报错（此前只上顶栏，
391|   * 展开行一直显示「暂无工具」，分不清没工具还是拉取失败） */

52|/** 一行一个参数 → string[]（trim、去空行） */
53|function parseArgsLines(text: string): string[] {
54|  return text
55|    .split("\n")
56|    .map((l) => l.trim())
57|    .filter((l) => l.length > 0);
58|}
59|
60|/** KEY=VALUE / Key: Value 行 → Record 通用内核（首个分隔符切；缺分隔符行取整行为 key、空值） */
61|function parseKeyValueLines(text: string, sep: string): Record<string, string> {
62|  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
63|  for (const line of text.split("\n")) {
64|    const t = line.trim();
65|    if (!t) continue;
66|    const i = t.indexOf(sep);
67|    if (i < 0) {
68|      out[t] = "";
69|    } else {
70|      out[t.slice(0, i).trim()] = t.slice(i + 1).trim();
71|    }
72|  }
73|  return out;
74|}
75|
76|/** 一行一个 KEY=VALUE → Record */
77|const parseEnvLines = (text: string) => parseKeyValueLines(text, "=");
78|
79|/** Key: Value 行 → Record（HTTP 头格式） */
80|const parseHeaderLines = (text: string) => parseKeyValueLines(text, ":");
81|
82|/** 命令行展示（B0 评审 HIGH）：含空白的参数加引号，让展示 token 与实际 argv 对齐
83| *（argv 是单 token 的 "hello world" 不能被展示成两个 shell 形态的词） */
84|function displayCommandLine(command: string | null | undefined, args: string[]): string {
85|  return [command ?? "", ...args]
86|    .map((a) => (/\s/.test(a) ? JSON.stringify(a) : a))
87|    .join(" ");
88|}
89|
90|/** 头值打码（B0-4）：确认弹窗只露末 4 位，不展示完整鉴权机密 */
91|function maskHeaderValue(v: string): string {
92|  const t = v.trim();
93|  return t.length <= 8 ? "••••" : `••••••${t.slice(-4)}`;
94|}
95|
96|/** 超时输入解析（B0-4）：空 = 默认；非法/越界报错（后端 u64 反序列化对
97| * 负数/小数会裸抛 serde 错误，前端先钳为 5–600 整数） */
98|function parseTimeoutSecs(raw: string): { value: number | null; error?: string } {
99|  const t = raw.trim();
100|  if (!t) return { value: null };
101|  const n = Number(t);
102|  if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 5 || n > 600) {
103|    return { value: null, error: "超时须为 5–600 的整数秒" };
104|  }
105|  return { value: n };
106|}
107|
108|/** 状态点：connected 绿 / down 红（tooltip 带错误）/ absent 灰 */
109|function StateDot({ status }: { status: McpServerStatus }) {
110|  const map: Record<string, { bg: string; tip: string }> = {
111|    connected: { bg: "bg-emerald-500", tip: "已连接" },
112|    down: { bg: "bg-red-500", tip: `不可用：${status.error ?? "未知错误"}` },
113|    absent: { bg: "bg-neutral-400", tip: status.enabled ? "未连接（启动中或等待）" : "已禁用" },
114|  };
115|  const m = map[status.state] ?? map.absent;
116|  return <span className={`size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full ${m.bg}`} title={m.tip} />;
117|}
118|
119|/** 行内错误摘要：stderr 尾巴可能有多行（评审口径），列表行只显示前 80 字符 */
120|function errorSummary(err: string | null | undefined): string {
121|  const oneLine = (err ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
122|  return oneLine.length > 80 ? `${oneLine.slice(0, 80)}…` : oneLine;
123|}
124|
125|/** 保存确认弹窗内容（拍板 3A）：完整命令行 / URL + env，用户看得见才点得下去 */
126|function SaveConfirmDialog({
127|  server,
128|  onCancel,
129|  onConfirm,
130|  busy,
131|}: {
132|  server: McpServerConfig;
133|  onCancel: () => void;
134|  onConfirm: () => void;
135|  busy: boolean;
136|}) {
137|  const isStdio = server.transport === "stdio";
138|  const envEntries = Object.entries(server.env ?? {});
139|  const headerEntries = Object.entries(server.headers ?? {});
140|  return (
141|    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 p-6">
142|      <div className="nm-card w-full max-w-lg p-5">
143|        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--t1)]">
144|          {server.id ? "保存修改" : "添加"} MCP 服务器「{server.name}」
145|        </h3>
146|        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--t5)]">
147|          {isStdio
148|            ? "该服务器是一个本地程序，wmessage 将以你的身份运行它。请确认下面这条命令是你预期的："
149|            : "wmessage 将通过 HTTP 连接该端点并调用其工具："}
150|        </p>
151|        <div className="mt-3 rounded bg-black/5 p-3 font-mono text-xs break-all text-[var(--t2)]">
152|          {isStdio ? displayCommandLine(server.command, server.args ?? []) : server.url}
153|        </div>
154|        {envEntries.length > 0 && (
155|          <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--t4)]">
156|            环境变量（存系统钥匙串，不明文写配置文件）：
157|            {envEntries.map(([k, v]) => (
158|              <div key={k} className="mt-0.5 font-mono break-all">
159|                {k}={v}
160|              </div>
161|            ))}
162|          </div>
163|        )}
164|        {headerEntries.length > 0 && (
165|          <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--t4)]">
166|            自定义头（值已打码，鉴权头等机密将随请求发送）：
167|            {headerEntries.map(([k, v]) => (
168|              <div key={k} className="mt-0.5 font-mono break-all">
169|                {k}: {maskHeaderValue(v)}
170|              </div>
171|            ))}
172|          </div>
173|        )}
174|        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--t5)]">
175|          该服务器的工具将进入机器人的工具清单，机器人可代你调用。env 与自定义头存系统钥匙串
176|          （不再明文写配置文件），删除或关闭开关即停用。
177|        </p>
178|        <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
179|          <button className="nm-btn px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-[var(--t3)]" onClick={onCancel}>
180|            取消
181|          </button>
182|          <button
183|            className="nm-btn px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-[var(--t1)] disabled:opacity-50"
184|            onClick={onConfirm}
185|            disabled={busy}
186|          >
187|            {busy ? "保存中…" : "确认，保存并连接"}
188|          </button>
189|        </div>
190|      </div>
191|    </div>
192|  );
193|}

195|/** MCP 服务器管理面板：列表（状态点/启停/工具）+ 表单（确认弹窗后保存） */
196|export function McpPanel() {
197|  const [servers, setServers] = useState<McpServerStatus[]>([]);
198|  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState | null>(null); // null = 表单收起
199|  const [pending, setPending] = useState<McpServerConfig | null>(null); // 确认弹窗
200|  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
201|  const [error, setError] = useState("");
202|  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
203|  /** 展开工具清单的服务器 id + 已拉取的工具（toolsError = 拉取失败，行内展示） */
204|  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
205|  const [tools, setTools] = useState<McpToolBrief[]>([]);
206|  const [toolsError, setToolsError] = useState("");
207|  const noticeTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
208|  useEffect(
209|    () => () => {
210|      if (noticeTimer.current) clearTimeout(noticeTimer.current);
211|    },
212|    []
213|  );
214|
215|  const flashNotice = useCallback((msg: string) => {
216|    setNotice(msg);
217|    if (noticeTimer.current) clearTimeout(noticeTimer.current);
218|    noticeTimer.current = setTimeout(() => setNotice(""), 3000);
219|  }, []);
220|
221|  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
222|    try {
223|      // ?? [] 防御：mock invoke / 后端异常时保持数组形态
224|      setServers((await invoke<McpServerStatus[]>("mcp_status")) ?? []);
225|    } catch (e) {
226|      handleCommandError(e, "mcp_status", { silent: true });
227|      setError(formatCommandError(e));
228|    }
229|  }, []);
230|
231|  /** 800ms 延迟刷新（重连异步收敛）：单句柄防抖（B0 评审：数组累积无上界），卸载清理 */
232|  const refreshTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
233|  useEffect(
234|    () => () => {
235|      if (refreshTimer.current) clearTimeout(refreshTimer.current);
236|    },
237|    []
238|  );
239|  const scheduleRefresh = useCallback(() => {
240|    if (refreshTimer.current) clearTimeout(refreshTimer.current);
241|    refreshTimer.current = setTimeout(refresh, 800);
242|  }, [refresh]);
243|
244|  useEffect(() => {
245|    refresh();
246|    // 连接态是异步收敛的（启动重连 / 保存后重连），后端广播后刷新状态点
247|    const un = listen("mcp-status-changed", () => {
248|      refresh();
249|    });
250|    return () => {
251|      un.then((f) => f());
252|    };
253|  }, [refresh]);
254|
255|  /** 编辑回填 in-flight 闸（B0 评审：连点两行会双 invoke 竞态 setForm——按钮的
256|   * disabled 要等首个 invoke 落地才生效，ref 闸同步无窗口） */
257|  const editLoadingRef = useRef(false);
258|  const startEdit = async (s: McpServerStatus) => {
259|    if (editLoadingRef.current || form !== null) return;
260|    editLoadingRef.current = true;
261|    setError("");
262|    // 表单回填需要原始配置字段——status 视图没有 args/env，
263|    // 从「编辑即整行覆盖」的角度只需回填用户可感知字段，args/env 留空会丢：
264|    // 因此编辑走 invoke bot_get_config 拿完整 mcpServers。
265|    try {
266|      const cfg = await invoke<{ mcpServers?: McpServerConfig[] }>("bot_get_config");
267|      const full = (cfg.mcpServers ?? []).find((x) => x.id === s.id);
268|      if (!full) {
269|        setError("找不到该服务器的完整配置");
270|        return;
271|      }
272|      setForm({
273|        id: full.id,
274|        name: full.name,
275|        transport: full.transport,
276|        command: full.command ?? "npx",
277|        argsText: (full.args ?? []).join("\n"),
278|        envText: Object.entries(full.env ?? {})
279|          .map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${v}`)
280|          .join("\n"),
281|        headersText: Object.entries(full.headers ?? {})
282|          .map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`)
283|          .join("\n"),
284|        url: full.url ?? "",
285|        timeoutSecs: full.timeoutSecs != null ? String(full.timeoutSecs) : "",
286|        enabled: full.enabled,
287|      });
288|    } catch (e) {
289|      handleCommandError(e, "bot_get_config", { silent: true });
290|      setError(formatCommandError(e));
291|    } finally {
292|      editLoadingRef.current = false;
293|    }
294|  };
295|
296|  /** 表单 → McpServerConfig（前端轻校验；后端权威校验兜底） */
297|  const buildPayload = (): McpServerConfig | null => {
298|    if (!form) return null;
299|    const name = form.name.trim();
300|    if (!name) {
301|      setError("名称不能为空");
302|      return null;
303|    }
304|    // stdio 的 command 来自白名单下拉（MCP_STDIO_LAUNCHERS）恒非空，无需守卫
305|    //（B0 评审：原 !form.command 分支不可达，删除）
306|    if (form.transport !== "stdio" && !form.url.trim()) {
307|      setError("http 服务器必须填写端点 URL");
308|      return null;
309|    }
310|    const timeout = parseTimeoutSecs(form.timeoutSecs);
311|    if (timeout.error) {
312|      setError(timeout.error);
313|      return null;
314|    }
315|    return {
316|      id: form.id,
317|      name,
318|      transport: form.transport,
319|      command: form.transport === "stdio" ? form.command : null,
320|      args: form.transport === "stdio" ? parseArgsLines(form.argsText) : [],
321|      env: form.transport === "stdio" ? parseEnvLines(form.envText) : {},
322|      url: form.transport === "http" ? form.url.trim() : null,
323|      headers: form.transport === "http" ? parseHeaderLines(form.headersText) : {},
324|      timeoutSecs: timeout.value,
325|      enabled: form.enabled,
326|    };
327|  };
328|
329|  const requestSave = () => {
330|    setError("");
331|    const payload = buildPayload();
332|    if (payload) setPending(payload); // 打开确认弹窗（拍板 3A）
333|  };
334|
335|  const confirmSave = async () => {
336|    if (!pending || busy) return;
337|    setBusy(true);
338|    setError("");
339|    try {
340|      await invoke<McpServerConfig[]>("mcp_server_save", { server: pending });
341|      setPending(null);
342|      setForm(null);
343|      flashNotice("已保存，正在连接…");
344|      // 后端 save 已触发重载；稍等一拍再刷状态（重载是异步的）
345|      scheduleRefresh();
346|      await refresh();
347|    } catch (e) {
348|      handleCommandError(e, "mcp_server_save", { silent: true });
349|      setError(formatCommandError(e));
350|      setPending(null); // 校验失败：关弹窗留表单，错误显示在表单里
351|    } finally {
352|      setBusy(false);
353|    }
354|  };
355|
356|  const remove = async (s: McpServerStatus) => {
357|    if (busy) return;
358|    if (!window.confirm(`删除 MCP 服务器「${s.name}」？其工具将立即从机器人清单消失。`)) return;
359|    setBusy(true);
360|    setError("");
361|    try {
362|      await invoke("mcp_server_delete", { id: s.id });
363|      flashNotice("已删除");
364|      await refresh();
365|    } catch (e) {
366|      handleCommandError(e, "mcp_server_delete", { silent: true });
367|      setError(formatCommandError(e));
368|    } finally {
369|      setBusy(false);
370|    }
371|  };
372|
373|  const toggle = async (s: McpServerStatus, enabled: boolean) => {
374|    if (busy) return;
375|    setBusy(true);
376|    setError("");
377|    try {
378|      await invoke("mcp_server_toggle", { id: s.id, enabled });
379|      await refresh();
380|      scheduleRefresh(); // 重连异步收敛
381|    } catch (e) {
382|      handleCommandError(e, "mcp_server_toggle", { silent: true });
383|      setError(formatCommandError(e));
384|    } finally {
385|      setBusy(false);
386|    }
387|  };
388|
389|  /** 展开/拉取工具：请求序号防串台（B0 评审：连点两台服务器，慢响应会把
390|   * A 的工具渲染进 B 的展开行）；失败在展开行内报错（此前只上顶栏，
391|   * 展开行一直显示「暂无工具」，分不清没工具还是拉取失败） */
392|  const toolsReqRef = useRef(0);
393|  const expandTools = async (s: McpServerStatus) => {
394|    if (expandedId === s.id) {
395|      setExpandedId(null);
396|      return;
397|    }
398|    const req = ++toolsReqRef.current;
399|    setExpandedId(s.id);
400|    setTools([]);
401|    setToolsError("");
402|    try {
403|      const t = await invoke<McpToolBrief[]>("mcp_server_tools", { id: s.id });
404|      if (toolsReqRef.current === req) setTools(t);
405|    } catch (e) {
406|      handleCommandError(e, "mcp_server_tools", { silent: true });
407|      if (toolsReqRef.current === req) setToolsError(formatCommandError(e));
408|    }
409|  };
410|
411|  return (
412|    <div className="nm-card p-5">
413|      <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--t1)]">MCP 服务器（外部工具）</h2>
414|      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--t5)]">
415|        连接外部 MCP 服务器，把它们的工具挂进机器人工具清单。启动器仅限
416|        {" "}{MCP_STDIO_LAUNCHERS.join(" / ")}；添加前会展示完整命令让你确认
417|      </p>
418|      <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2">
419|        <button
420|          className="nm-btn px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-[var(--t3)]"
421|          onClick={() => {
422|            setError("");
423|            setForm({ ...EMPTY_FORM });
424|          }}
425|          disabled={form !== null}
426|        >
427|          ＋ 添加服务器

430|        {error && <span className="text-xs text-[var(--danger)]">{error}</span>}
431|      </div>

433|      {/* 服务器列表 */}
434|      {servers.length === 0 && form === null ? (

Now I understand the IconButton. Let me analyze the changes: 1. The original first button had `shrink-0 w-5 h-5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs text-[var(--t5)] hover:text-[var(--danger)] hover:bg-[var(--hover-bg)]` 2. The new version is `w-5 h-5 rounded-full text-xs hover:text-[var(--danger)]` The IconButton's `nm-icon-btn` CSS provides: - `inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center` - replaces `flex items-center justify-center shrink-0` - `color: var(--t4)` - replaces `text-[var(--t5)]` (note: t4 vs t5 - close but different) - `:hover { color: var(--t2); background-color: var(--hover-bg); }` - replaces `hover:bg-[var(--hover-bg)]`, but `hover:text-[var(--danger)]` from className overrides the `:hover { color: var(--t2); }` Wait, CSS specificity issue! The `.nm-icon-btn:hover` rule sets `color: var(--t2)` and `background-color: var(--hover-bg)`. The className also adds `hover:text-[var(--danger)]`. Both are at the same specificity level. But since `.nm-icon-btn:hover` has a more specific selector (pseudo-class), it should win over a plain `.hover:text-[...]` class (which doesn't have a pseudo-class). Actually wait - Tailwind's `hover:` variants add a `:hover` pseudo-class too. So: - `.nm-icon-btn:hover { color: var(--t2) }` - specificity 0,1,1 - `.hover\:text-\[var\(--danger\)\]:hover { color: var(--danger) }` - specificity 0,2,0 So the Tailwind hover should win because two classes win over one class + pseudo-class. So the danger color should still apply on hover. But the background hover `bg-[var(--hover-bg)]` was removed because the IconButton's hover already provides that. Good. However, there's a subtle issue. The original code's first button had `text-[var(--t5)]` (lighter text) for the trash icon. The new code relies on `.nm-icon-btn { color: var(--t4) }` which is `t4` instead of `t5`. This is a slight visual change but probably intentional (more consistent). For the second/third buttons (edit/delete links), the original was `text-[var(--t4)]` which is now `t4` from `.nm-icon-btn` - matches. Now let me check if there are any other concerns. Request #4 MiniMax-M3 P:22.6K C:5.71K CR:21.01K CW:0 26142ms
Now I have enough information. Let me synthesize the findings. **Key findings:** 1. **Removed variables check**: Only old docs (DEVLOG.md, README.md) reference the removed `--sh-*` variables — those are just historical text, no live code references. Good. 2. **No `box-sizing` reset anywhere** in the codebase. Tailwind v3 preflight DOES set `box-sizing: border-box` via `*, ::before, ::after`, but I should check if it's overridden. Since the project uses `@tailwind base`, preflight should apply. So my concern about the 1px border causing layout shift is **mitigated** — borders will be absorbed into existing dimensions under border-box. 3. **`color-scheme` is properly declared** in both `:root` (light) and `.dark` (dark), and there's a test asserting this. The first `:root` block in the theme doesn't need color-scheme since the scrollbar section handles it. 4. **`.nm-icon-btn` has NO focus state** — confirmed by reading the IconButton component (no `:focus-visible` handling in JS either). Real keyboard accessibility gap. 5. **`.nm-input` focus** — only border-color change to brand. With `outline: none` and only border-color changing, this is indeed an a11y regression compared to browser defaults. 6. **`var(--t6)` usages**: Used in text-[10px] / text-xs / border-t lines (legal, valid use), but the new value `#bcc5d6` (light) and `#545e70` (dark) against the new backgrounds. Contrast check needed: - Light: `#bcc5d6` vs `#eef1f6` — contrast ~1.3:1 (very poor) - Dark: `#545e70` vs `#14161d` — better, maybe acceptable 7. **`--inset-bg` at 0.045 opacity**: On near-white `#fafbfd`, the dark overlay `rgba(23, 32, 51, 0.045)` produces only ~3% darkness — barely perceptible as a depression cue. Confirmed by the diff: the entire "凹下/按压" semantic now relies on this almost-invisible tint, which weakens a major interaction signal used by `.nm-inset` (selected/pressed state) and `.nm-btn:active`. 8. **Many call sites for `.nm-card` (~30+)**: Used in dialogs (max-w-md/max-w-lg/max-w-2xl with `p-4`/`p-5`/`p-6`), kanban board, todo cards, etc. These will all gain a 1px border (absorbed under border-box, no layout shift, but visible outline added — this is the intended visual change). 9. **`.nm-input` has 8 call sites in McpPanel** — real forms will use this. Border-color-only focus is a regression vs the original neumorphic design (which had shadow-based focus via box-shadow changes that were more visible). Now I have enough to provide a thorough review. Let me deliver the feedback. ## Summary This is a major CSS theme redesign in `src/ui/main.css`, replacing the neumorphic ("新拟态") implementation with a flat, layered Linear-style design ("U1"). It removes the old light/dark shadow variables (`--sh-low`, `--sh-high`, `--sh-low-strong`, `--sh-high-strong`, `--sh-top`), introduces new tokens (surface tiers, edges, single-layer shadows, border-radius/font-scale), and rewrites every component class (`nm-card`, `nm-inset`, `nm-outset`, `nm-btn`, `nm-sidebar-panel*`) under the same names so consumers don't need code changes. New utilities `.nm-icon-btn`, `.nm-input`, `.nm-tag` are added — the first one backs the new `IconButton` component. I verified (a) no live CSS/TS code still references the deleted `--sh-*` variables (only old docs mention them), (b) the project uses Tailwind v3 preflight so `box-sizing: border-box` is global — the new 1px card borders won't cause layout shifts, and (c) `color-scheme` is correctly declared for both themes with an existing test asserting it. Below are the issues worth flagging in the newly added code. --- ### Issues #### 1. `.nm-input:focus` removes the default outline with only a brand-color border as the focus cue — keyboard a11y regression **Severity:** medium · **Category:** accessibility The new `.nm-input:focus { outline: none; border-color: var(--brand); }` (lines 220–222) replaces what was previously a more visible neumorphic shadow-based focus. On the redesigned near-white surface, a 1px brand-colored border is a very subtle focus indicator — and there is no `:focus-visible` ring, no glow, no shadow lift. With 8+ real form inputs in `McpPanel.tsx` now wired to `.nm-input` (e.g. lines 529, 536, 547, 559, 567, 578, 585, 595), keyboard users tabbing through the settings panel will get a much weaker focus signal than before. Suggest adding an explicit focus ring (e.g. `box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--brand) 25%, transparent);`) or an `:focus-visible` outline that meets WCAG 2.4.7. ```css .nm-input:focus-visible { outline: none; border-color: var(--brand); box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--brand) 25%, transparent); } ``` #### 2. `.nm-icon-btn` has no `:focus` / `:focus-visible` state — keyboard a11y gap for the new `IconButton` **Severity:** medium · **Category:** accessibility The new icon-button base class (lines 195–209) defines `:hover`, `:active`, `:disabled` but nothing for `:focus` or `:focus-visible`. I checked `src/ui/IconButton.tsx` (the only consumer introduced in this change) — it doesn't add any focus styling either; it just spreads `...rest` props onto the `<button>`. Icon buttons are typically used in toolbars and headers (the JSDoc explicitly mentions "扁平材质（无底色，hover 淡底提字）") where there's no surrounding color to differentiate a focused button from an unfocused one. Keyboard tab order will be invisible. Add at least: ```css .nm-icon-btn:focus-visible { outline: none; background-color: var(--hover-bg); color: var(--t2); box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px var(--brand); } ``` #### 3. `--inset-bg` is almost invisible in light mode — the new "凹下/按压" affordance is weakened **Severity:** medium · **Category:** maintainability The light-mode `--inset-bg: rgba(23, 32, 51, 0.045)` (line 28) produces roughly 3% darkening on `--surface: #fafbfd` — barely perceptible to the eye. Both `.nm-inset` (the new "选中/凹陷" state, lines 165–170) and `.nm-btn:active` (line 192) rely on this single token as their depression cue. Compared with the old neumorphic `.nm-inset` (two inset shadows of ±8px that gave a strong tactile "凹" reading), the new selected/pressed state will look almost identical to the resting state — collapsing a semantic distinction that many call sites rely on (theme/board/archive/workspace/trash/settings view toggles in `App.tsx:627-670`, the "推理强度" four-position selector and "字号" four-position selector in `SettingsPage.test.tsx:319-864`, `EvolutionPanel.tsx:300`, `KanbanBoard.tsx:61`, `MigrationPanel.tsx:178`, etc.). Consider bumping light-mode `--inset-bg` to `~0.08` or pairing it with the `--edge-strong` border so the depressed state is unambiguously distinguishable. #### 4. Light-theme `--t6: #bcc5d6` likely fails WCAG contrast against the new `--bg` / `--surface` **Severity:** medium · **Category:** accessibility The new light `--t6: #bcc5d6` (line 47) against the new `--bg: #eef1f6` / `--surface: #fafbfd` yields a contrast ratio around 1.3:1 — far below WCAG AA's 4.5:1 for normal text. The token is named "幽灵提示" (ghost hint) but is consumed in many real locations, not just decoration: - `text-[var(--t6)]` at `text-[10px]` / `text-xs` in `SettingsPage.tsx` (8 occurrences: 1086, 1102, 1153, 1186, 1203, 1243, 1285, 1315) - `text-[var(--t6)]` at `text-xs` in `TodoCard.tsx:333, 387` (interactive links with `hover:text-[var(--t3)]`) - `border-t border-[var(--t6)]` in `MigrationPanel.tsx:175` The old light `--t6: #c7ced8` was already light; the new value `#bcc5d6` is lighter still and against a lighter `--bg`. Settings-page helper text at 10–13px is exactly the scenario where readability matters. Either darken `--t6` to `#8e99b0`-ish territory, or split into two tokens (`--t6` decorative vs a `--t6-text` guaranteed-readable). #### 5. `.nm-card-hover:hover` lost the `translateY/scale` lift — interactive cards now feel flat **Severity:** low · **Category:** maintainability / UX The old `.nm-card-hover:hover` had `transform: translateY(-3px) scale(1.01)` plus stronger dual shadows (lines removed). The new version (lines 146–151) is purely a `background-color` + `border-color` swap with no transform. `TodoCard.tsx:236`, `SortableTaskCard.tsx`, `SortableWorkspaceCard.tsx`, `WorkspacePage.tsx:564` all use `.nm-card-hover` for primary draggable/clickable cards. Without the lift, hover discoverability — especially in the widget where cards are smaller and more tightly packed — will feel like the card "does nothing" on hover. The diff's own animation-disciple comment ("无弹跳") explicitly forbids the lift, but a 1–2px translateY combined with the new `--shadow-lg` would still read as "lift" without being "弹跳". Worth reconsidering, or at least adding a `box-shadow: var(--shadow-lg)` swap on hover so the card visibly raises. #### 6. Inconsistent transition timings within a single card rule and against the documented discipline **Severity:** low · **Category:** style The diff's design comment promises "卡片 150ms，按钮/Chips 100ms，无弹跳", but: - `.nm-card` base (line 138) uses `0.15s` ✓ - `.nm-card-hover:hover` (line 150) uses `0.15s` ✓ - `.nm-btn`/`.nm-outset`/`.nm-inset` (lines 182, 175, 168) use `0.1s` ✓ - `.nm-sidebar-panel` / `.nm-sidebar-panel-top` (lines 251, 256) still use `0.28s ease` for background-color — this is consistent with the body theme-switch transition, but the contrast against the new 100–150ms rhythm is jarring when toggling theme while a sidebar is visible. Consider unifying on 0.15s for the background-color portion since the body transition already handles the theme switch visually. #### 7. `.nm-card` / `.nm-inset` / `.nm-outset` ignore the new `--r-*` tokens, hard-coding Tailwind rounded utilities **Severity:** low · **Category:** maintainability The new `:root` block adds `--r-sm: 8px`, `--r-md: 12px`, `--r-lg: 16px` (lines 51–53) "新组件取 var；存量 Tailwind rounded-*/text-* 不动" — the intent is for *new* components to consume these tokens. But the component classes themselves still hard-code via `@apply rounded-2xl` / `rounded-xl` / `rounded-lg` (lines 134, 165, 172, 181). If a designer wants to retune the global corner radius (e.g. switch the whole product to softer 14px / 20px), the three nm-* classes must be hand-edited regardless. Either wire them up to the tokens (`@apply rounded-[var(--r-lg)]`) or drop the tokens if they're truly for new code only — currently they're in a half-applied state. #### 8. `.nm-tag` lacks hover/focus/active states **Severity:** low · **Category:** maintainability The new `.nm-tag` (lines 230–237) is a static pill with border + text color. If it's ever used as an interactive element (filter chips, tag pickers), there's no press feedback. Either document it as static-only via the JSDoc, or add `:hover` / `:active` mirroring `.nm-btn` so the system stays consistent. --- ### Confirmed non-issues (so reviewers don't have to re-check) - ✅ Removed `--sh-*` variables have **no** live references in CSS/TS code (only stale historical docs in `DEVLOG.md` / `README.md`). - ✅ The 1px border added to `.nm-card` / `.nm-card-hover` does **not** cause layout shifts: Tailwind v3 preflight (`@tailwind base`) sets `box-sizing: border-box` globally. - ✅ `color-scheme: light` / `color-scheme: dark` is correctly declared in the scrollbar section, with an existing test (`main-css.test.ts:13-16`) asserting both — no missing `.dark { color-scheme: dark }`. - ✅ `IconButton.tsx` correctly uses `type={type}` defaulting to `"button"` to prevent form-submit accidents. --- Let me submit the actionable code comments for the new-code issues:

391|   chat 输入框（text-xs）、通知/计数（text-xs）大量用 Tailwind 标准类，
392|   原方案下切档无视觉差异 → 用户误以为没生效。
393|   - small = 默认不动（老板拍板「目前字号为小」）
